Clause / section / page: 10.3.1.2 / Part I-A Chapter 10 - Ship communication / 28

Original source text:
Ships intended to provide icebreaking escort shall be equipped with a sound signaling system mounted to face astern to indicate escort and emergency manoeuvres to following ships as described in the International Code of Signals.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_provides_icebreaking_escort = true, THEN astern_facing_sound_signaling_system_present = true AND International_Code_of_Signals_compliance_status = compliant.
Required inputs: Escort role, signaling-system installation, and code-compliance evidence

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO094Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO094NonEscortBranch
        gen:IMO094EscortBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO094NonEscortBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipProvidesIcebreakingEscort ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "The ship's icebreaking-escort applicability must be recorded as false when the escort-specific obligations do not apply."
    ] .

gen:IMO094EscortBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipProvidesIcebreakingEscort ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship's icebreaking-escort applicability must be recorded as true for the escort-specific obligations to apply."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:asternFacingSoundSignalingSystemPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "A ship providing icebreaking escort must have an astern-facing sound signaling system."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:internationalCodeOfSignalsComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "A ship providing icebreaking escort must comply with the International Code of Signals."
    ] .

```

For ships exposed to ice accretion, means shall be provided to remove or prevent ice and snow accretion from escape routes, muster stations, embarkation areas, survival craft, their launching appliances and access to survival craft.

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO066ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO066NotExposedBranch
        gen:IMO066ExposedBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO066NotExposedBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipExposedToIceAccretion ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:message "The ship's ice-accretion applicability must be recorded as false when this conditional requirement is not applicable."
    ] .

gen:IMO066ExposedBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipExposedToIceAccretion ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship's ice-accretion applicability must be recorded as true when this conditional requirement applies."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasProtectedItem ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO066ProtectedItemShape ;
        sh:message "For a ship exposed to ice accretion, protected-item inventory and associated ice or snow removal or prevention means are required."
    ] .

gen:IMO066ProtectedItemShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:protectedItem ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:protectedItemCategory ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:escapeRouteProtectedItem
            nltl:musterStationProtectedItem
            nltl:embarkationAreaProtectedItem
            nltl:survivalCraftProtectedItem
            nltl:launchingApplianceProtectedItem
            nltl:survivalCraftAccessProtectedItem
        ) ;
        sh:message "Each protected item must identify one permitted escape, muster, embarkation, survival-craft, launching-appliance, or survival-craft-access category."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIceSnowRemovalOrPreventionMeans ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:iceSnowRemovalOrPreventionMeans
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each protected item must have associated means to remove or prevent ice and snow accretion."
    ] .

```

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-040-20260915T104518815561Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
For ships operating in areas and during periods where ice accretion is likely to occur, means shall be provided to remove or prevent ice and snow accretion around hatches and doors.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_accretion_likely = true, THEN every relevant hatch and door shall have an associated ice_or_snow_removal_or_prevention_means.
Required inputs: Hatch and door inventory and associated anti-icing means

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_040_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:message "The ice accretion applicability value must be false or true."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceAccretionLikely ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "The ice accretion applicability value must be false or true."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Every relevant hatch or door must have an associated ice or snow removal or prevention means when ice accretion is likely." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:iceAccretionLikely true .
                        $this nltl:hasRelevantHatchOrDoor ?hatch .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?hatch nltl:hasIceOrSnowRemovalOrPreventionMeans ?means .
                            ?means rdf:type nltl:iceSnowRemovalOrPreventionMeans .
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        }
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:engagedInTrade false ;
    nltl:grossTonnage ex:grossTonnageValue ;
    nltl:lengthOverall ex:lengthOverallValue ;
    nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable true ;
    nltl:shipType "cargo_ship"^^xsd:string .

ex:grossTonnageValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 500.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:lengthOverallValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 24.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26-002-ShipShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship type is required to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Chapter 9-1 applicability must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lengthOverall ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:grossTonnage ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engagedInTrade ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Chapter 9-1 applicability must equal the applicable branch for the ship type and required dimensions, tonnage, and trade status." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipType ?type .
    $this nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ?app .
    FILTER (
        (
            ?type = "fishing_vessel" &&
            (
                !EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue .
                    ?lengthValue qudt:numericValue ?lengthNumber ;
                                  qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?lengthNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                }
                ||
                (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue2 .
                        ?lengthValue2 qudt:numericValue ?lengthNumber2 ;
                                   qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?lengthNumber2) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    &&
                    (
                        (
                            EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue3 .
                                ?lengthValue3 qudt:numericValue ?lengthNumber3 ;
                                           qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                                FILTER (datatype(?lengthNumber3) = xsd:decimal && ?lengthNumber3 >= 24)
                            }
                            && ?app != true
                        )
                        ||
                        (
                            !EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue4 .
                                ?lengthValue4 qudt:numericValue ?lengthNumber4 ;
                                           qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                                FILTER (datatype(?lengthNumber4) = xsd:decimal && ?lengthNumber4 >= 24)
                            }
                            && ?app != false
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        )
        ||
        (
            ?type = "pleasure_yacht" &&
            (
                !EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue .
                    ?grossValue qudt:numericValue ?grossNumber ;
                                qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?grossNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                }
                ||
                !EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade .
                    FILTER (datatype(?trade) = xsd:boolean)
                }
                ||
                (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue2 .
                        ?grossValue2 qudt:numericValue ?grossNumber2 ;
                                     qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?grossNumber2) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    &&
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade2 .
                        FILTER (datatype(?trade2) = xsd:boolean)
                    }
                    &&
                    (
                        (
                            EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue3 .
                                ?grossValue3 qudt:numericValue ?grossNumber3 ;
                                             qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                                FILTER (datatype(?grossNumber3) = xsd:decimal && ?grossNumber3 >= 300)
                            }
                            &&
                            EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade3 .
                                FILTER (datatype(?trade3) = xsd:boolean && ?trade3 = false)
                            }
                            && ?app != true
                        )
                        ||
                        (
                            !(
                                EXISTS {
                                    $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue4 .
                                    ?grossValue4 qudt:numericValue ?grossNumber4 ;
                                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                                    FILTER (datatype(?grossNumber4) = xsd:decimal && ?grossNumber4 >= 300)
                                }
                                &&
                                EXISTS {
                                    $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade4 .
                                    FILTER (datatype(?trade4) = xsd:boolean && ?trade4 = false)
                                }
                            )
                            && ?app != false
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        )
        ||
        (
            ?type = "cargo_ship" &&
            (
                !EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:grossTonnage ?cargoGrossValue .
                    ?cargoGrossValue qudt:numericValue ?cargoGrossNumber ;
                                      qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    FILTER (datatype(?cargoGrossNumber) = xsd:decimal)
                }
                ||
                (
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:grossTonnage ?cargoGrossValue2 .
                        ?cargoGrossValue2 qudt:numericValue ?cargoGrossNumber2 ;
                                       qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                        FILTER (datatype(?cargoGrossNumber2) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    &&
                    (
                        (
                            EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:grossTonnage ?cargoGrossValue3 .
                                ?cargoGrossValue3 qudt:numericValue ?cargoGrossNumber3 ;
                                                   qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                                FILTER (datatype(?cargoGrossNumber3) = xsd:decimal &&
                                        ?cargoGrossNumber3 >= 300 &&
                                        ?cargoGrossNumber3 < 500)
                            }
                            && ?app != true
                        )
                        ||
                        (
                            !EXISTS {
                                $this nltl:grossTonnage ?cargoGrossValue4 .
                                ?cargoGrossValue4 qudt:numericValue ?cargoGrossNumber4 ;
                                                   qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                                FILTER (datatype(?cargoGrossNumber4) = xsd:decimal &&
                                        ?cargoGrossNumber4 >= 300 &&
                                        ?cargoGrossNumber4 < 500)
                            }
                            && ?app != false
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        )
        ||
        (
            ?type NOT IN ("fishing_vessel", "pleasure_yacht", "cargo_ship") &&
            ?app != false
        )
    )
}
"""
    ] .
```

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] .

gen:NonApplicableBranch a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A non-applicable result is permitted only when ship type is not one of the Chapter 9-1 applicability types." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipType ?type .
                FILTER (?type IN ("fishing_vessel", "pleasure_yacht", "cargo_ship"))
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:Chapter9Dash1ApplicabilityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Ship type is required to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Chapter 9-1 applicability must be recorded as a single boolean result."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:FishingVesselBranch
        gen:PleasureYachtBranch
        gen:CargoShipBranch
        gen:NonApplicableBranch
    ) .

```

```turtle
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship type is required to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engagedInTrade ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Trade status is required to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lengthOverall ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt-unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Length overall is required in metres to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:grossTonnage ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt-unit:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Gross tonnage is required as a dimensionless quantity to determine Chapter 9-1 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Chapter 9-1 applicability must match the prescribed ship type, dimensions, tonnage, and trade-status conditions."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Chapter 9-1 applicability must be true exactly for qualifying fishing vessels, pleasure yachts, and cargo ships." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX qudt-unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
          nltl:engagedInTrade ?tradeStatus ;
          nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthQuantity ;
          nltl:grossTonnage ?tonnageQuantity ;
          nltl:polarCodeChapter9Dash1Applicable ?applicable .

    ?lengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?lengthValue ;
                    qudt:unit qudt-unit:M .
    ?tonnageQuantity qudt:numericValue ?tonnageValue ;
                     qudt:unit qudt-unit:UNITLESS .

    BIND(
        (
            (STR(?shipType) = "fishing_vessel" &&
             ?lengthValue >= 24)
            ||
            (STR(?shipType) = "pleasure_yacht" &&
             ?tonnageValue >= 300 &&
             ?tradeStatus = false)
            ||
            (STR(?shipType) = "cargo_ship" &&
             ?tonnageValue >= 300 &&
             ?tonnageValue < 500)
        ) AS ?qualifies
    )

    FILTER(
        (?applicable = true && ?qualifies = false)
        ||
        (?applicable = false && ?qualifies = true)
    )
}
"""
    ] .
```

Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?

Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?

```turtle
gen:IMO082ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:solasRegulationV22Point1Point9Point4ComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The ship shall comply with SOLAS regulation V/22.1.9.4."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bridgeConfigurationType ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:bridgeConfigurationValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The bridge configuration shall be provided to determine whether a clear view astern is required."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Where the bridge configuration requires an astern view, clear view astern status shall be recorded as compliant." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bridgeConfigurationType
                      nltl:asternViewRequiredBridgeConfiguration .
                FILTER (
                    !EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:clearViewAsternStatus
                              "true"^^xsd:boolean
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:clearViewAsternStatus ?status .
                        FILTER (?status != "true"^^xsd:boolean)
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:message "The bridge configuration shall be provided to determine whether a clear astern view is required."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When the bridge configuration requires an astern view, clear view astern status shall be true." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bridgeConfigurationType
                    nltl:asternViewRequiredBridgeConfiguration .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:clearViewAsternStatus ?status .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?status) = xsd:boolean &&
                        ?status = true
                    )
                }
            }
        """
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-085-Static> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in ( nltl:polarShipCategoryA nltl:polarShipCategoryB nltl:polarShipCategoryC ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A valid ship category is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A valid construction date is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bridgeWingsEnclosed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bridgeWingProtectionDesignStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, the bridge wings must be enclosed or the bridge-wing protection design must be approved." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?date .
                FILTER (?category IN (
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                    nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                ))
                FILTER (datatype(?date) = xsd:date)
                FILTER (
                    YEAR(?date) > 2017 ||
                    (YEAR(?date) = 2017 && MONTH(?date) > 1) ||
                    (YEAR(?date) = 2017 && MONTH(?date) = 1 && DAY(?date) >= 1)
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:bridgeWingsEnclosed true .
                }
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:bridgeWingProtectionDesignStatus true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
For ships operating in low air temperature, a Polar Service Temperature (PST) shall be specified and shall be at least 10 deg C below the lowest MDLT for the intended area and season of operation in polar waters. Systems and equipment required by this Code shall be fully functional at the PST.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_operates_in_low_air_temperature = true, THEN PST_degC shall be specified, PST_degC <= lowest_MDLT_degC - 10, and required_systems_functional_at_PST = true.
Required inputs: PST, lowest MDLT, equipment inventory, and functional-verification status

```turtle
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_019_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature must be specified as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarServiceTemperature ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Polar service temperature must be specified as a single temperature quantity in degrees Celsius."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:lowestMdlt ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Lowest MDLT must be specified as a single temperature quantity in degrees Celsius."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:requiredSystemsFunctionalAtPst ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [

        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
